first hop redirects to the local TLS endpoint. I will verify the certificate before requesting that destination.                                                                                                        ⚙ hercules_workspace_read_file [path=artifacts/redirect.txt]I will validate the actual chain with the supplied lab CA, preserving OpenSSL diagnostics.⚙ hercules_shell_exec [command=openssl s_client -connect 172.30.80.10:8443 -CAfile /opt/workspace/lab-cert.pem -verify_return_error </dev/null > /opt/workspace/artifacts/tls.txt 2>&1; cat /opt/workspace/artifacts/tls.txt,  timeout=45]                                                                                                                                                                                                                 The lab chain verifies. I will request the TLS page with certificate validation enabled and retain the response.⚙ hercules_network_curl [url=https://172.30.80.10:8443/login, extra_args=--cacert /opt/workspace/lab-cert.pem --max-time 5 -o /opt/workspace/artifacts/tls-login.html]                                                                                                               Before browser operations, I will read Hercules's actual browser guidance and use fresh snapshots for targeting.⚙ hercules_browser_skill                                                                                        I will observe how the browser handles the redirect. A supplied curl CA does not automatically establish browser trust.                                                                                                                 ⚙ hercules_browser_open [url=http://172.30.80.10:8080, session=review]                                                                     I will check the live local page in the native browser⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝ backend rather th⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝an infer behavior from headers.⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝⬝                                                                                                        ⚙ hercules_workspace_read_file [path=artifacts/redirect.txt]I will validate the actual chain with the supplied lab CA, preserving OpenSSL diagnostics.⚙ hercules_shell_exec [command=openssl s_client -connect 172.30.80.10:8443 -CAfile /opt/workspace/lab-cert.pem -verify_return_error </dev/null > /opt/workspace/artifacts/tls.txt 2>&1; cat /opt/workspace/artifacts/tls.txt,  timeout=45]                                                                                                                                                                                                                 The lab chain verifies.
